<p><b>South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands</b> (SGSSI) is a <a href="/wiki/British_Overseas_Territories" title="British Overseas Territories">British Overseas Territory</a> in the southern <a href="/wiki/Atlantic_Ocean" title="Atlantic Ocean">Atlantic Ocean</a>. It is a remote and inhospitable collection of islands, consisting of <a href="/wiki/South_Georgia" title="South Georgia">South Georgia</a> and a chain of smaller islands known as the South Sandwich Islands. South Georgia is 165 kilometres (103&#160;mi) long and 35 kilometres (22&#160;mi) wide and is by far the largest island in the territory. The South Sandwich Islands lie about 700 kilometres (430&#160;mi) southeast of South Georgia. The territory's total land area is 3,903&#160;km<sup>2</sup> (1,507&#160;sq&#160;mi).<sup id="cite_ref-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-1">&#91;1&#93;</a></sup> The <a href="/wiki/Falkland_Islands" title="Falkland Islands">Falkland Islands</a> are about 1,300 kilometres (810&#160;mi) west from its nearest point.
</p><p>The South Sandwich Islands are uninhabited, and a very small non-permanent population resides on South Georgia.<sup id="cite_ref-population_2-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-population-2">&#91;2&#93;</a></sup> There are no scheduled passenger flights or ferries to or from the territory, although visits by cruise liners to South Georgia are increasingly popular, with several thousand visitors each summer.
</p><p>The United Kingdom claimed <a href="/wiki/Sovereignty" title="Sovereignty">sovereignty</a> over South Georgia in 1775 and the South Sandwich Islands in 1908. The territory of "South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands" was formed in 1985;<sup id="cite_ref-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-3">&#91;3&#93;</a></sup> previously, it had been governed as part of the <a href="/wiki/Falkland_Islands_Dependencies" title="Falkland Islands Dependencies">Falkland Islands Dependencies</a>. <a href="/wiki/Argentina" title="Argentina">Argentina</a> claimed South Georgia in 1927 and claimed the South Sandwich Islands in 1938.
</p><p>Argentina maintained a naval station, <a href="/wiki/Corbeta_Uruguay" class="mw-redirect" title="Corbeta Uruguay">Corbeta Uruguay</a>, on <a href="/wiki/Thule_Island" title="Thule Island">Thule Island</a> in the South Sandwich Islands from 1976 until 1982 when it was closed by the <a href="/wiki/Royal_Navy" title="Royal Navy">Royal Navy</a>. The Argentine claim over South Georgia contributed to the 1982 <a href="/wiki/Falklands_War" title="Falklands War">Falklands War</a>, during which Argentine forces briefly occupied the island. Argentina <a href="/wiki/South_Georgia_and_South_Sandwich_Islands_sovereignty_dispute" class="mw-redirect" title="South Georgia and South Sandwich Islands sovereignty dispute">continues to claim sovereignty</a> over South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands as part of the <a href="/wiki/Tierra_del_Fuego,_Ant%C3%A1rtida_e_Islas_del_Atl%C3%A1ntico_Sur_Province" class="mw-redirect" title="Tierra del Fuego, Antártida e Islas del Atlántico Sur Province">Tierra del Fuego, Antártida e Islas del Atlántico Sur Province</a>.
</p><p><a href="/wiki/Dissostichus" title="Dissostichus">Toothfish</a> are vital to the islands' economy; as a result, <a href="/wiki/Toothfish_Day" title="Toothfish Day">Toothfish Day</a> is celebrated on 4 September as a <a href="/wiki/Bank_holiday" title="Bank holiday">bank holiday</a> in the territory.<sup id="cite_ref-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-4">&#91;4&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<meta property="mw:PageProp/toc" />
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="History">History</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: History">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1033289096">.mw-parser-output .hatnote{font-style:italic}.mw-parser-output div.hatnote{padding-left:1.6em;margin-bottom:0.5em}.mw-parser-output .hatnote i{font-style:normal}.mw-parser-output .hatnote+link+.hatnote{margin-top:-0.5em}</style><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/History_of_South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands" title="History of South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands">History of South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands</a></div>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="South_Georgia">South Georgia</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: South Georgia">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="17th_to_19th_centuries">17th to 19th centuries</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: 17th to 19th centuries">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Seale-1744.png" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/ef/Seale-1744.png/220px-Seale-1744.png" decoding="async" width="220" height="161" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/ef/Seale-1744.png/330px-Seale-1744.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/ef/Seale-1744.png/440px-Seale-1744.png 2x" data-file-width="800" data-file-height="587" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Seale-1744.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>Richard William Seale's map of 1744, showing Roche Island and noting its discovery in 1675</div></div></div>
<p>The island of South Georgia was first sighted in 1675 by <a href="/wiki/Anthony_de_la_Roch%C3%A9" title="Anthony de la Roché">Anthony de la Roché</a>, a London merchant and (despite his French name) an Englishman.<sup id="cite_ref-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-5">&#91;5&#93;</a></sup> The island appeared as <i>Roche Island</i> on early maps.<sup id="cite_ref-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-6">&#91;6&#93;</a></sup> The commercial Spanish ship <i>León</i>, operating out of <a href="/wiki/Saint-Malo" title="Saint-Malo">Saint-Malo</a> sighted it on 28 June or 29 June 1756.<sup id="cite_ref-Iberoamerica_–_Bienvenido_7-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Iberoamerica_–_Bienvenido-7">&#91;7&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p><a href="/wiki/James_Cook" title="James Cook">James Cook</a> <a href="/wiki/Second_voyage_of_James_Cook" title="Second voyage of James Cook">circumnavigated the island</a> in 1775 and made the first landing. He claimed the territory for the <a href="/wiki/Kingdom_of_Great_Britain" title="Kingdom of Great Britain">Kingdom of Great Britain</a>, naming it the "Isle of Georgia" in honour of King <a href="/wiki/George_III_of_the_United_Kingdom" class="mw-redirect" title="George III of the United Kingdom">George III of the United Kingdom</a>. British arrangements for the government of South Georgia were established under 1843 British <a href="/wiki/Letters_patent" title="Letters patent">letters patent</a>.
</p><p>In 1882–1883 a <a href="/wiki/German_Empire" title="German Empire">German</a> expedition for the first <a href="/wiki/International_Polar_Year" title="International Polar Year">International Polar Year</a> set up its base at <a href="/wiki/Royal_Bay" title="Royal Bay">Royal Bay</a> on the southeast side of the island. The scientists of this group observed the <a href="/wiki/Transit_of_Venus" title="Transit of Venus">transit of Venus</a> and recorded waves produced by the <a href="/wiki/1883_eruption_of_Krakatoa" title="1883 eruption of Krakatoa">1883 eruption of Krakatoa</a>. <a href="/wiki/Seal_hunting" title="Seal hunting">Seal hunting</a> at South Georgia began in 1786 and continued throughout the 19th century. The waters proved treacherous and a number of vessels were wrecked there, such as <a href="/wiki/Earl_Spencer_(1799_ship)" title="Earl Spencer (1799 ship)"><i>Earl Spencer</i></a>, in late 1801.<sup id="cite_ref-8" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-8">&#91;8&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="20th_and_21st_centuries">20th and 21st centuries</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: 20th and 21st centuries">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Grytviken_church.jpg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/31/Grytviken_church.jpg/220px-Grytviken_church.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="165" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/31/Grytviken_church.jpg/330px-Grytviken_church.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/31/Grytviken_church.jpg/440px-Grytviken_church.jpg 2x" data-file-width="800" data-file-height="600" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Grytviken_church.jpg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div><a href="/wiki/Norwegian_Anglican_Church,_Grytviken" title="Norwegian Anglican Church, Grytviken">Norwegian Lutheran Church</a> at <a href="/wiki/Grytviken" title="Grytviken">Grytviken</a></div></div></div>
<p>South Georgia became a base for <a href="/wiki/Whaling" title="Whaling">whaling</a> beginning in the 20th century. A Norwegian, <a href="/wiki/Carl_Anton_Larsen" title="Carl Anton Larsen">Carl Anton Larsen</a>, established the first land-based whaling station and first permanent habitation at <a href="/wiki/Grytviken" title="Grytviken">Grytviken</a> in 1904. It operated through his <a href="/wiki/Compa%C3%B1%C3%ADa_Argentina_de_Pesca" title="Compañía Argentina de Pesca">Argentine Fishing Company</a>, which settled in Grytviken.<sup id="cite_ref-Iberoamerica_–_Bienvenido_7-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Iberoamerica_–_Bienvenido-7">&#91;7&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-9" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-9">&#91;9&#93;</a></sup> The station operated until 1965. <a href="/wiki/Whaling_station" class="mw-redirect" title="Whaling station">Whaling stations</a> operated under leases granted by the Governor of the <a href="/wiki/Falkland_Islands" title="Falkland Islands">Falkland Islands</a>. The seven stations, all on the north coast with its sheltered harbours, were, from the west to east:
</p>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r998391716">.mw-parser-output .div-col{margin-top:0.3em;column-width:30em}.mw-parser-output .div-col-small{font-size:90%}.mw-parser-output .div-col-rules{column-rule:1px solid #aaa}.mw-parser-output .div-col dl,.mw-parser-output .div-col ol,.mw-parser-output .div-col ul{margin-top:0}.mw-parser-output .div-col li,.mw-parser-output .div-col dd{page-break-inside:avoid;break-inside:avoid-column}</style><div class="div-col">
<ol><li><a href="/wiki/Prince_Olav_Harbour" title="Prince Olav Harbour">Prince Olav Harbour</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Leith_Harbour" title="Leith Harbour">Leith Harbour</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Stromness_(South_Georgia)" class="mw-redirect" title="Stromness (South Georgia)">Stromness</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Husvik" title="Husvik">Husvik</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Grytviken" title="Grytviken">Grytviken</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Godthul" title="Godthul">Godthul</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Ocean_Harbour" title="Ocean Harbour">Ocean Harbour</a></li></ol>
</div>
<p>The whaling stations' <a href="/wiki/Trywork" title="Trywork">tryworks</a> were unpleasant and dangerous places to work. One was called "a <a href="/wiki/Charnel_house" title="Charnel house">charnel house</a> boiling wholesale in Vaseline" by an early 20th-century visitor. <a href="/wiki/Tim_Flannery" title="Tim Flannery">Tim Flannery</a> wrote that its "putrid vapors [resembled] the pong of bad fish, manure, and a tanning works mixed together", and noted one bizarre peril: "A rotting whale could fill with gas to bursting, ejecting a fetus the size of a motor vehicle with sufficient force to kill a man."<sup id="cite_ref-10" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-10">&#91;10&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:PanoramaOfSouthGeorgia.jpg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c2/PanoramaOfSouthGeorgia.jpg/220px-PanoramaOfSouthGeorgia.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="84" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c2/PanoramaOfSouthGeorgia.jpg/330px-PanoramaOfSouthGeorgia.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c2/PanoramaOfSouthGeorgia.jpg/440px-PanoramaOfSouthGeorgia.jpg 2x" data-file-width="792" data-file-height="303" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:PanoramaOfSouthGeorgia.jpg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>South Georgia, photographed by <a href="/wiki/Frank_Hurley" title="Frank Hurley">Frank Hurley</a> during the <a href="/wiki/Imperial_Trans-Antarctic_Expedition" title="Imperial Trans-Antarctic Expedition">Imperial Trans-Antarctic Expedition</a></div></div></div>
<p>With the end of the whaling industry, the stations were abandoned. Apart from a few preserved buildings such as the <a href="/wiki/South_Georgia_Museum" title="South Georgia Museum">South Georgia Museum</a> and <a href="/wiki/Norwegian_Anglican_Church,_Grytviken" title="Norwegian Anglican Church, Grytviken">Norwegian Lutheran Church at Grytviken</a>, only their decaying remains survive. From 1905, the Argentine Meteorological Office cooperated in maintaining a meteorological observatory at Grytviken under the British lease requirements of the whaling station until these changed in 1949.
</p><p>In 1908, the United Kingdom issued further <a href="/wiki/Letters_patent" title="Letters patent">letters patent</a> that established constitutional arrangements for its possessions in the South Atlantic. The letters covered South Georgia, the <a href="/wiki/South_Orkneys" class="mw-redirect" title="South Orkneys">South Orkneys</a>, the <a href="/wiki/South_Shetlands" class="mw-redirect" title="South Shetlands">South Shetlands</a>, the South Sandwich Islands, and <a href="/wiki/Graham_Land" title="Graham Land">Graham Land</a>. The claim was extended in 1917 to include a sector of Antarctica reaching to the <a href="/wiki/South_Pole" title="South Pole">South Pole</a>. In 1909, an administrative centre and residence were established at King Edward Point on South Georgia, near the whaling station of Grytviken. A permanent local British administration and resident magistrate exercised effective <a href="/wiki/Possession_(law)" title="Possession (law)">possession</a>, enforcement of <a href="/wiki/United_Kingdom_law" class="mw-redirect" title="United Kingdom law">British law</a>, and regulation of all economic, scientific, and other activities in the territory, which was then governed as the <a href="/wiki/Falkland_Islands_Dependencies" title="Falkland Islands Dependencies">Falkland Islands Dependencies</a>. In about 1912, what is according to some accounts the largest whale ever caught, a <a href="/wiki/Blue_whale" title="Blue whale">blue whale</a> of 110 feet (34&#160;m), was landed at Grytviken.<sup id="cite_ref-11" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-11">&#91;11&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-12" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-12">&#91;12&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>In April 1916, <a href="/wiki/Ernest_Shackleton" title="Ernest Shackleton">Ernest Shackleton</a>'s <a href="/wiki/Imperial_Trans-Antarctic_Expedition" title="Imperial Trans-Antarctic Expedition">Imperial Trans-Antarctic Expedition</a> became stranded on <a href="/wiki/Elephant_Island" title="Elephant Island">Elephant Island</a>, some 800 miles (1,300&#160;km) southwest of South Georgia. Shackleton and five companions set out in a small boat to summon help, and on 10 May, after an epic voyage, they landed at <a href="/wiki/King_Haakon_Bay" title="King Haakon Bay">King Haakon Bay</a> on South Georgia's south coast. While three stayed at the coast, Shackleton and the two others, <a href="/wiki/Tom_Crean_(explorer)" title="Tom Crean (explorer)">Tom Crean</a> and <a href="/wiki/Frank_Worsley" title="Frank Worsley">Frank Worsley</a>, went on to cover 22 miles (35&#160;km) over the spine of the mountainous island to reach help at <a href="/wiki/Stromness,_South_Georgia" title="Stromness, South Georgia">Stromness</a> whaling station. The remaining 22 members of the expedition, who had stayed on Elephant Island, were subsequently rescued. In January 1922, during a <a href="/wiki/Shackleton%E2%80%93Rowett_Expedition" title="Shackleton–Rowett Expedition">later expedition</a>, Shackleton died on board ship while moored in King Edward Cove, South Georgia. He is buried at Grytviken. The ashes of another noted Antarctic explorer, <a href="/wiki/Frank_Wild" title="Frank Wild">Frank Wild</a>, who had been Shackleton's second-in-command on the Imperial Trans-Antarctic Expedition, were interred next to Shackleton in 2011.<sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;">&#91;<i><a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (October 2022)">citation needed</span></a></i>&#93;</sup>
</p>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:SG-Settlements.png" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/57/SG-Settlements.png/220px-SG-Settlements.png" decoding="async" width="220" height="160" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/57/SG-Settlements.png/330px-SG-Settlements.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/57/SG-Settlements.png/440px-SG-Settlements.png 2x" data-file-width="3702" data-file-height="2684" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:SG-Settlements.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>Historical and modern settlements of South Georgia Island</div></div></div>
<p>Argentina claimed South Georgia in 1927.<sup id="cite_ref-13" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-13">&#91;13&#93;</a></sup> The basis of this claim and of a later claim in 1938 to the South Sandwich Islands has been questioned.<sup id="cite_ref-14" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-14">&#91;14&#93;</a></sup> During the <a href="/wiki/World_War_II" title="World War II">Second World War</a>, the Royal Navy deployed an armed merchant vessel to patrol South Georgian and Antarctic waters against German raiders, along with two four-inch shore guns (still present) protecting Cumberland Bay and Stromness Bay, which were operated by volunteers from among the Norwegian whalers. The base at King Edward Point was expanded as a research facility in 1949–1950 by the <a href="/wiki/British_Antarctic_Survey" title="British Antarctic Survey">British Antarctic Survey</a>, which until 1962 was called the Falkland Islands Dependencies Survey.
</p><p>The <a href="/wiki/Falklands_War" title="Falklands War">Falklands War</a> was precipitated on 19 March 1982 when a group of Argentinians (most of them <a href="/wiki/Argentine_Marines" class="mw-redirect" title="Argentine Marines">Argentine Marines</a> in <a href="/wiki/Mufti_(dress)" title="Mufti (dress)"><i>mufti</i></a>), posing as scrap-metal merchants, occupied the abandoned whaling station at <a href="/wiki/Leith_Harbour" title="Leith Harbour">Leith Harbour</a> on South Georgia. On 3 April, Argentine troops <a href="/wiki/Battle_of_Grytviken" class="mw-redirect" title="Battle of Grytviken">attacked and occupied Grytviken</a>. Among the commanding officers of the Argentine garrison was <a href="/wiki/Alfredo_Astiz" title="Alfredo Astiz">Alfredo Astiz</a>, a captain in the <a href="/wiki/Argentine_Navy" title="Argentine Navy">Argentine Navy</a> who was convicted years later of crimes against humanity committed during the <a href="/wiki/Dirty_War" title="Dirty War">Dirty War</a> in Argentina. The island was recaptured by British forces on 25 April, in <a href="/wiki/Operation_Paraquet" title="Operation Paraquet">Operation Paraquet</a>.
</p><p>In 1985, South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands ceased to be administered as a Falkland Islands Dependency and became a separate territory. The <a href="/wiki/King_Edward_Point" title="King Edward Point">King Edward Point</a> base, which had become a small military garrison after the Falklands War, returned to civilian use in 2001 and is now operated by the <a href="/wiki/British_Antarctic_Survey" title="British Antarctic Survey">British Antarctic Survey</a>.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="South_Sandwich_Islands">South Sandwich Islands</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5" title="Edit section: South Sandwich Islands">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>Captain <a href="/wiki/James_Cook" title="James Cook">James Cook</a> discovered the southern eight islands of the Sandwich Islands Group in 1775, although he lumped the southernmost three together, and their status as separate islands was not established until 1820 by <a href="/wiki/Fabian_Gottlieb_von_Bellingshausen" title="Fabian Gottlieb von Bellingshausen">Fabian Gottlieb von Bellingshausen</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-15" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-15">&#91;15&#93;</a></sup> The northern three islands were discovered by Bellingshausen in 1819. The islands were tentatively named "Sandwich Land" by Cook, although he also commented that they might be a group of islands rather than a single body of land. The name was chosen in honour of <a href="/wiki/John_Montagu,_4th_Earl_of_Sandwich" title="John Montagu, 4th Earl of Sandwich">John Montagu, 4th Earl of Sandwich</a>, who was First Lord of the Admiralty. The word "South" was later added to distinguish them from the "Sandwich Islands", now known as the <a href="/wiki/Hawaiian_Islands" title="Hawaiian Islands">Hawaiian Islands</a>.
</p><p>Argentina claimed the South Sandwich Islands in 1938, and challenged British sovereignty in the Islands on several occasions. From 25 January 1955 to mid-1956, Argentina maintained the summer station Teniente Esquivel at <a href="/wiki/Ferguson_Bay" title="Ferguson Bay">Ferguson Bay</a> on the southeastern coast of <a href="/wiki/Thule_Island" title="Thule Island">Thule Island</a>. Argentina maintained a naval base (<a href="/wiki/Corbeta_Uruguay" class="mw-redirect" title="Corbeta Uruguay">Corbeta Uruguay</a>) from 1976 to 1982, in the lee (southern east coast) of the same island. Although the British discovered the presence of the Argentine base in 1976,<sup id="cite_ref-Freedman2005_16-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Freedman2005-16">&#91;16&#93;</a></sup> protested and tried to resolve the issue by diplomatic means, no effort was made to remove them by force until after the <a href="/wiki/Falklands_War" title="Falklands War">Falklands War</a>. The base was removed on 20 June 1982.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Geography">Geography</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=6" title="Edit section: Geography">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Map_of_South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands.svg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/7a/Map_of_South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands.svg/220px-Map_of_South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands.svg.png" decoding="async" width="220" height="156" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/7a/Map_of_South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands.svg/330px-Map_of_South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/7a/Map_of_South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands.svg/440px-Map_of_South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="1052" data-file-height="744" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Map_of_South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands.svg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>Map of the islands</div></div></div>
<p>South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands are a collection of islands in the South Atlantic Ocean. Most of the islands, rising steeply from the sea, are rugged and mountainous. At higher elevations, the islands are permanently covered with ice and snow.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="South_Georgia_Group">South Georgia Group</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=7" title="Edit section: South Georgia Group">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>The South Georgia Group lies about 1,390 kilometres (860&#160;mi; 750&#160;nmi) east-southeast of the Falkland Islands, at 54°–55°S, 36°–38°W. It comprises <a href="/wiki/South_Georgia_(island)" class="mw-redirect" title="South Georgia (island)">South Georgia Island</a> itself, by far the largest island in the territory, and the islands that immediately surround it and some remote and isolated islets to the west and east-southeast. It has a total land area of 3,756 square kilometres (1,450&#160;sq&#160;mi), including satellite islands, but excluding the South Sandwich Islands which form a separate island group.
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Islands_within_the_South_Georgia_Group">Islands within the South Georgia Group</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=8" title="Edit section: Islands within the South Georgia Group">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:1989_Grytvikken_hg.jpg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/00/1989_Grytvikken_hg.jpg/220px-1989_Grytvikken_hg.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="138" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/00/1989_Grytvikken_hg.jpg/330px-1989_Grytvikken_hg.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/00/1989_Grytvikken_hg.jpg/440px-1989_Grytvikken_hg.jpg 2x" data-file-width="2439" data-file-height="1527" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:1989_Grytvikken_hg.jpg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div><a href="/wiki/Grytviken" title="Grytviken">Grytviken</a></div></div></div>
<p>South Georgia Island lies at <link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1073938472"/><span class="plainlinks nourlexpansion"><a class="external text" href="//geohack.toolforge.org/geohack.php?pagename=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;params=54_15_S_36_45_W_scale:2500000"><span class="geo-default"><span class="geo-dms" title="Maps, aerial photos, and other data for this location"><span class="latitude">54°15′S</span> <span class="longitude">36°45′W</span></span></span><span class="geo-multi-punct">&#xfeff; / &#xfeff;</span><span class="geo-nondefault"><span class="geo-dec" title="Maps, aerial photos, and other data for this location">54.250°S 36.750°W</span><span style="display:none">&#xfeff; / <span class="geo">-54.250; -36.750</span></span></span></a></span><span style="font-size: small;"><span id="coordinates"><a href="/wiki/Geographic_coordinate_system" title="Geographic coordinate system">Coordinates</a>: <link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1073938472"/><span class="plainlinks nourlexpansion"><a class="external text" href="//geohack.toolforge.org/geohack.php?pagename=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;params=54_15_S_36_45_W_scale:2500000"><span class="geo-default"><span class="geo-dms" title="Maps, aerial photos, and other data for this location"><span class="latitude">54°15′S</span> <span class="longitude">36°45′W</span></span></span><span class="geo-multi-punct">&#xfeff; / &#xfeff;</span><span class="geo-nondefault"><span class="geo-dec" title="Maps, aerial photos, and other data for this location">54.250°S 36.750°W</span><span style="display:none">&#xfeff; / <span class="geo">-54.250; -36.750</span></span></span></a></span></span></span> and has an area of 3,528 square kilometres (1,362&#160;sq&#160;mi). It is mountainous and largely barren. Eleven peaks rise to over 2,000 metres (6,600&#160;ft) high, their slopes furrowed with deep gorges filled with glaciers; the largest is <a href="/wiki/Fortuna_Glacier" title="Fortuna Glacier">Fortuna Glacier</a>. The highest peak is <a href="/wiki/Mount_Paget" title="Mount Paget">Mount Paget</a> in the <a href="/wiki/Allardyce_Range" title="Allardyce Range">Allardyce Range</a> at 2,934 metres (9,626&#160;ft).
</p><p>Geologically, the island consists of <a href="/wiki/Gneiss" title="Gneiss">gneiss</a> and <a href="/wiki/Argillaceous_schist" title="Argillaceous schist">argillaceous schists</a> with occasional tuffs and other sedimentary layers from which fossils have been recovered.<sup id="cite_ref-trendall_17-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-trendall-17">&#91;17&#93;</a></sup> The island is a fragment of some greater land-mass now vanished and was probably a former extension of the <a href="/wiki/Andes" title="Andes">Andean system</a>.
</p><p>Smaller islands and islets off the coast of South Georgia Island include:
</p>
<div>
<table class="multicol" role="presentation" style="border-collapse: collapse; padding: 0; border: 0; background:transparent; width:66%;">

<tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: left; vertical-align: top;">
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Annenkov_Island" title="Annenkov Island">Annenkov Island</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Bird_Island_(South_Georgia)" class="mw-redirect" title="Bird Island (South Georgia)">Bird Island</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Cooper_Island" title="Cooper Island">Cooper Island</a></li></ul>
</td>
<td style="text-align: left; vertical-align: top;">
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Grass_Island_(South_Georgia)" class="mw-redirect" title="Grass Island (South Georgia)">Grass Island</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Jomfruene" title="Jomfruene">Jomfruene</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Pickersgill_Islands" title="Pickersgill Islands">Pickersgill Islands</a></li></ul>
</td>
<td style="text-align: left; vertical-align: top;">

</p>
</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p>These remote rocks are also considered part of the South Georgia Group:
</p>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Shag_Rocks_(South_Georgia)" title="Shag Rocks (South Georgia)">Shag Rocks</a>, 185&#160;km (115&#160;mi; 100&#160;nmi) west-northwest of South Georgia Island</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Black_Rock_(South_Georgia)" class="mw-redirect" title="Black Rock (South Georgia)">Black Rock</a>, 169&#160;km (105&#160;mi; 91&#160;nmi) west-northwest of South Georgia Island</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Clerke_Rocks" title="Clerke Rocks">Clerke Rocks</a>, 56&#160;km (35&#160;mi; 30&#160;nmi) east-southeast of South Georgia Island</li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="South_Sandwich_Islands_2">South Sandwich Islands</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=9" title="Edit section: South Sandwich Islands">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:South_Sandwich_Islands_Map.svg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/9f/South_Sandwich_Islands_Map.svg/220px-South_Sandwich_Islands_Map.svg.png" decoding="async" width="220" height="328" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/9f/South_Sandwich_Islands_Map.svg/330px-South_Sandwich_Islands_Map.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/9f/South_Sandwich_Islands_Map.svg/440px-South_Sandwich_Islands_Map.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="494" data-file-height="737" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:South_Sandwich_Islands_Map.svg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>Closeup map of the South Sandwich Islands</div></div></div>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Montagu_Island_Satellite_Photograph.jpg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/38/Montagu_Island_Satellite_Photograph.jpg/220px-Montagu_Island_Satellite_Photograph.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="202" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/38/Montagu_Island_Satellite_Photograph.jpg/330px-Montagu_Island_Satellite_Photograph.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/38/Montagu_Island_Satellite_Photograph.jpg/440px-Montagu_Island_Satellite_Photograph.jpg 2x" data-file-width="500" data-file-height="460" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Montagu_Island_Satellite_Photograph.jpg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>NASA satellite photograph of Montagu Island</div></div></div>
<p>The South Sandwich Islands (<a href="/wiki/Spanish_language" title="Spanish language">Spanish</a>: <i lang="es">Islas Sandwich del Sur</i>) comprise 11 mostly <a href="/wiki/Volcano" title="Volcano">volcanic</a> islands (excluding tiny satellite islands and offshore rocks), with some active volcanoes. They form an <a href="/wiki/Island_arc" title="Island arc">island arc</a> running north–south in the region 56°18'–59°27'S, 26°23'–28°08'W, between about 350 and 500&#160;mi (300 and 430&#160;nmi; 560 and 800&#160;km) southeast of South Georgia.
</p><p>The northernmost of the South Sandwich Islands form the <a href="/wiki/Traversay_Islands" title="Traversay Islands">Traversay Islands</a> and <a href="/wiki/Candlemas_Islands" title="Candlemas Islands">Candlemas Islands</a> groups, while the southernmost make up <a href="/wiki/Southern_Thule" title="Southern Thule">Southern Thule</a>. The three largest islands&#160;&#8211;&#32;<a href="/wiki/Saunders_Island,_South_Sandwich_Islands" title="Saunders Island, South Sandwich Islands">Saunders</a>, <a href="/wiki/Montagu_Island" title="Montagu Island">Montagu</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Bristol_Island" title="Bristol Island">Bristol</a>&#160;&#8211;&#32;lie between the two. The Islands' highest point is <a href="/wiki/Mount_Belinda" title="Mount Belinda">Mount Belinda</a> (1,370&#160;m or 4,495&#160;ft) on Montagu Island. The fourth highest peak, <a href="/wiki/Mount_Michael" title="Mount Michael">Mount Michael</a> (990&#160;m or 3,248&#160;ft) on Saunders Island has a persistent <a href="/wiki/Lava_lake" title="Lava lake">lava lake</a>, known to occur at only eight volcanoes in the world.<sup id="cite_ref-Nytimes2019-07-12_18-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Nytimes2019-07-12-18">&#91;18&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-19" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-19">&#91;19&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>The South Sandwich Islands are uninhabited, though a permanently staffed Argentine research station was located on Thule Island from 1976 to 1982 (for details, see <a href="#History">§&#160;History</a> above). Automatic <a href="/wiki/Weather_station" title="Weather station">weather stations</a> are on Thule Island and <a href="/wiki/Zavodovski_Island" title="Zavodovski Island">Zavodovski</a>. To the northwest of Zavodovski Island is the <a href="/wiki/Protector_Shoal" title="Protector Shoal">Protector Shoal</a>, a submarine volcano.
</p>

<td><b>South Sandwich Islands</b><br /><i><span style="font-size:85%;">(entire)</span></i>
</td>
<td align="right">310&#160;km<sup>2</sup> (120&#160;sq&#160;mi)</td>
<td><a href="/wiki/Mount_Belinda" title="Mount Belinda">Mount Belinda</a><br />1,370&#160;m (4,490&#160;ft)</td>
<td>
</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>A series of six passages separates each of the islands or island groups in the chain. They are, from north to south: Zavodovski Isl., <i>Traverse passage</i>, Visokoi Isl., <i>Brown's passage</i>, Candlemas Isl., <i>Shackleton's passage</i>, Saunders Isl., <i>Larsen's passage</i>, Montagu Isl., Biscoe's passage, Bristol Isl., <a href="/wiki/Forsters_Passage" title="Forsters Passage">Forsters Passage</a>, Southern Thule. Nelson Channel is the passage between Candlemas and Vindication Island.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Extreme_points">Extreme points</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=10" title="Edit section: Extreme points">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li>Northernmost point – <a href="/wiki/Cape_North_(South_Georgia)" title="Cape North (South Georgia)">Cape North</a></li>
<li>Southernmost point – on <a href="/wiki/Cook_Island,_South_Sandwich_Islands" title="Cook Island, South Sandwich Islands">Cook Island</a></li>
<li>Westernmost point – on <a href="/wiki/Main_Island,_Willis_Islands" class="mw-redirect" title="Main Island, Willis Islands">Main Island</a> (of the <a href="/wiki/Willis_Islands" title="Willis Islands">Willis Islands</a>)</li>
<li>Easternmost point – on <a href="/wiki/Montagu_Island" title="Montagu Island">Montagu Island</a></li>
<li>Highest point – <a href="/wiki/Mount_Paget" title="Mount Paget">Mount Paget</a>: 2,934 m</li>
<li>Lowest point – Atlantic Ocean: 0</li></ul>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Climate">Climate</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=11" title="Edit section: Climate">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:SouthGeorgiaIsland-EO.JPG" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/42/SouthGeorgiaIsland-EO.JPG/220px-SouthGeorgiaIsland-EO.JPG" decoding="async" width="220" height="152" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/42/SouthGeorgiaIsland-EO.JPG/330px-SouthGeorgiaIsland-EO.JPG 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/42/SouthGeorgiaIsland-EO.JPG/440px-SouthGeorgiaIsland-EO.JPG 2x" data-file-width="876" data-file-height="606" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:SouthGeorgiaIsland-EO.JPG" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>NASA satellite image of South Georgia Island covered with snow</div></div></div>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Wave_Clouds_from_South_Sandwich_Islands.jpg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/43/Wave_Clouds_from_South_Sandwich_Islands.jpg/220px-Wave_Clouds_from_South_Sandwich_Islands.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="286" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/43/Wave_Clouds_from_South_Sandwich_Islands.jpg/330px-Wave_Clouds_from_South_Sandwich_Islands.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/43/Wave_Clouds_from_South_Sandwich_Islands.jpg/440px-Wave_Clouds_from_South_Sandwich_Islands.jpg 2x" data-file-width="2000" data-file-height="2600" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Wave_Clouds_from_South_Sandwich_Islands.jpg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The South Sandwich Islands connect with air currents to make <a href="/wiki/Wave_cloud" title="Wave cloud">wave patterns</a> in clouds.</div></div></div>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Royal_Bay_and_South_Georgia_Island.jpg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/ee/Royal_Bay_and_South_Georgia_Island.jpg/220px-Royal_Bay_and_South_Georgia_Island.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="147" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/ee/Royal_Bay_and_South_Georgia_Island.jpg/330px-Royal_Bay_and_South_Georgia_Island.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/ee/Royal_Bay_and_South_Georgia_Island.jpg/440px-Royal_Bay_and_South_Georgia_Island.jpg 2x" data-file-width="720" data-file-height="480" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Royal_Bay_and_South_Georgia_Island.jpg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>Royal Bay and South Georgia Island</div></div></div>
<p>The climate is classified as polar, and the weather is highly variable and harsh, making a <a href="/wiki/Tundra" title="Tundra">tundra</a> (<a href="/wiki/Tundra" title="Tundra">ET</a>) in <a href="/wiki/K%C3%B6ppen_climate_classification" title="Köppen climate classification">Köppen climate classification</a>. Typical daily maximum temperatures in South Georgia at sea level are around 0&#160;°C (32&#160;°F) in winter (August) and 8&#160;°C (46.4&#160;°F) in summer (January). Winter minimum temperatures are typically about −5&#160;°C (23&#160;°F) and rarely dip below −10&#160;°C (14&#160;°F). Annual precipitation in South Georgia is about 1,500&#160;mm (59.1&#160;in), much of which falls as sleet or snow, which is possible the entire year. Inland, the snow line in summer is at an altitude of about 300&#160;m (984&#160;ft).
</p><p>Westerly winds blow throughout the year interspersed with periods of calm—indeed, in 1963, 25% of winds were in the calm category at King Edward Point, and the mean wind speed of around 8 knots (9.2&#160;mph; 15&#160;km/h) is around half that of the Falkland Islands. This gives the eastern side of South Georgia (leeward side) a more pleasant climate than the exposed western side. The prevailing weather conditions generally make the islands difficult to approach by ship, though the north coast of South Georgia has several large bays which provide good anchorage.
</p><p>Sunshine, as with many South Atlantic Islands, is low, at a maximum of just 21.5%. This amounts to around 1,000 hours of sunshine annually. The local <a href="/wiki/Topography" title="Topography">topography</a>, however, also contributes significantly to the low <a href="/wiki/Insolation" class="mw-redirect" title="Insolation">insolation</a>. A study published during the early 1960s<sup id="cite_ref-20" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-20">&#91;20&#93;</a></sup> indicated that sunshine recording instruments remained significantly obscured throughout the year and entirely obscured during June. It was estimated that the theoretical sunshine exposure minus obstructions would be around 14% at Bird Island and 35% at King Edward Point&#160;&#8211;&#32;or, in hourly terms, ranging from around 650 hours in the west to 1,500 hours in the east. This illustrates the effect the Allardyce Range has in breaking up cloud cover.
</p><p>Mountain winds rise over the western slopes of the mountains of South Georgia and down the eastern side and become much warmer and drier due to the <a href="/wiki/Foehn_wind" title="Foehn wind">Föhn effect</a>; this produces the most pleasant conditions when temperatures can occasionally rise to over 20&#160;°C (68&#160;°F) on summer days. The highest temperature recorded at the King Edward Point meteorological station (often generically and less accurately called Grytviken) on the sheltered eastern side of South Georgia is 28.8&#160;°C (83.8&#160;°F).<sup id="cite_ref-21" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup> Conversely, the highest recorded temperature at Bird Island on the windward western side is a mere 14.5&#160;°C (58.1&#160;°F). As one might expect, the sheltered eastern side can also record lower winter temperatures—the absolute minimum temperature for King Edward Point is −19.4&#160;°C (−2.9&#160;°F), but Bird Island just −11.4&#160;°C (11.5&#160;°F).
</p><p>The seas surrounding South Georgia are cold throughout the year due to the proximity of the <a href="/wiki/Antarctic_Circumpolar_Current" title="Antarctic Circumpolar Current">Antarctic Circumpolar Current</a>. They usually remain free of pack ice in winter, though thin ice may form in sheltered bays, and <a href="/wiki/Iceberg" title="Iceberg">icebergs</a> are common.<sup id="cite_ref-22" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-22">&#91;22&#93;</a></sup> Sea temperatures drop to 0&#160;°C (32&#160;°F) in late August and rise to around 4&#160;°C (39.2&#160;°F) only in early April.
</p><p>The South Sandwich Islands are much colder than South Georgia, being farther south and more exposed to cold outbreaks from the Antarctic continent. They are also surrounded by sea ice from the middle of May to late November (even longer at their southern end).<sup id="cite_ref-23" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-23">&#91;23&#93;</a></sup> Recorded temperature extremes at South Thule Island have ranged from −29.8 to 17.7&#160;°C (−21.6 to 63.9&#160;°F).
</p>

<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Outline_of_political_science#Politics_by_region" title="Outline of political science">Other countries</a></li></ul>
</div></td></tr><tr><td class="sidebar-navbar"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1129693374"/><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1063604349">.mw-parser-output .navbar{display:inline;font-size:88%;font-weight:normal}.mw-parser-output .navbar-collapse{float:left;text-align:left}.mw-parser-output .navbar-boxtext{word-spacing:0}.mw-parser-output .navbar ul{display:inline-block;white-space:nowrap;line-height:inherit}.mw-parser-output .navbar-brackets::before{margin-right:-0.125em;content:"[ "}.mw-parser-output .navbar-brackets::after{margin-left:-0.125em;content:" ]"}.mw-parser-output .navbar li{word-spacing:-0.125em}.mw-parser-output .navbar a>span,.mw-parser-output .navbar a>abbr{text-decoration:inherit}.mw-parser-output .navbar-mini abbr{font-variant:small-caps;border-bottom:none;text-decoration:none;cursor:inherit}.mw-parser-output .navbar-ct-full{font-size:114%;margin:0 7em}.mw-parser-output .navbar-ct-mini{font-size:114%;margin:0 4em}</style><div class="navbar plainlinks hlist navbar-mini"><ul><li class="nv-view"><a href="/wiki/Template:Politics_of_South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands" title="Template:Politics of South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands"><abbr title="View this template">v</abbr></a></li><li class="nv-talk"><a href="/wiki/Template_talk:Politics_of_South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands" title="Template talk:Politics of South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands"><abbr title="Discuss this template">t</abbr></a></li><li class="nv-edit"><a class="external text" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Template:Politics_of_South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit"><abbr title="Edit this template">e</abbr></a></li></ul></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>Executive power is vested in the <a href="/wiki/Monarchy_of_the_United_Kingdom" title="Monarchy of the United Kingdom">monarch of the United Kingdom</a> and is exercised by the <a href="/wiki/Commissioner_for_South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands" title="Commissioner for South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands">Commissioner</a>, a post held by the <a href="/wiki/Governor_of_the_Falkland_Islands" title="Governor of the Falkland Islands">Governor of the Falkland Islands</a>. The current Commissioner is <a href="/wiki/Alison_Blake" title="Alison Blake">Alison Blake</a>, who took the post on 1 July 2022.
</p><p>The executive, based in Stanley, Falkland Islands, made up of a Chief Executive, three Directors, two managers, and a Business Support Officer. 
</p><p>The Financial Secretary and Attorney General of the territory are appointed <i>ex officio</i> similar appointments in the Falkland Islands' government.
</p><p>On the island itself, Government Officers manage vessel visits, fishing and tourism, and represent the government 'on the ground'. A summer Deputy Postmaster runs the Post Office at Grytviken during the tourism season.
</p><p>As no permanent inhabitants live on the islands, no <a href="/wiki/Legislative_council" title="Legislative council">legislative council</a> and no elections are needed. The UK <a href="/wiki/Foreign,_Commonwealth_and_Development_Office" title="Foreign, Commonwealth and Development Office">Foreign, Commonwealth and Development Office</a> (FCDO) manages the foreign relations of the territory. Since 1982, the territory celebrates <a href="/wiki/Liberation_Day" title="Liberation Day">Liberation Day</a> on 25 April. 
</p><p>The constitution of the territory (adopted 3 October 1985), the manner in which its government is directed and the availability of judicial review were discussed in a series of litigations between 2001 and 2005 (see, in particular, <i>Regina v. Secretary of State for Foreign and Commonwealth Affairs (Appellant) ex parte Quark Fishing Limited</i> [2005] UKHL 57<sup id="cite_ref-28" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-28">&#91;28&#93;</a></sup>). Although its government is directed by the FCDO, it was held that, since it was acting as an agent of <a href="/wiki/The_Crown#Divisiblity_of_the_Crown" title="The Crown">the Crown</a> in right of South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands rather than in right of the UK, its decisions under that direction could not be challenged as if they were in law decisions of a UK government department;<sup class="noprint Inline-Template" style="margin-left:0.1em; white-space:nowrap;">&#91;<i><a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Please_clarify" title="Wikipedia:Please clarify"><span title="The text near this tag may need clarification or removal of jargon. (April 2014)">clarification needed</span></a></i>&#93;</sup> thus the European Convention on Human Rights did not apply.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Economy">Economy</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=13" title="Edit section: Economy">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p>Commercial <a href="/wiki/Seal_hunting" title="Seal hunting">sealing</a> occurred on the islands between 1817 and 1909. During that period 20 visits are recorded by sealing vessels.<sup id="cite_ref-29" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-29">&#91;29&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>Economic activity in South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands is limited. The territory has revenues of £6.3&#160;million, 80% of which is derived from fishing licences (2020 figures).<sup id="cite_ref-30" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-30">&#91;30&#93;</a></sup> Other sources of revenue are the sale of postage stamps and coins, tourism, and customs and harbour dues.<sup id="cite_ref-31" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-31">&#91;31&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Fishing">Fishing</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=14" title="Edit section: Fishing">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>Fishing takes place around South Georgia and in adjacent waters in some months of the year, with fishing licences sold by the territory for <a href="/wiki/Patagonian_toothfish" title="Patagonian toothfish">Patagonian toothfish</a>, <a href="/wiki/Nototheniidae" title="Nototheniidae">cod icefish</a> and <a href="/wiki/Krill" title="Krill">krill</a>. Fishing licences bring in millions of pounds a year, most of which is spent on fishery protection and research. All fisheries are regulated and managed in accordance with the <a href="/wiki/Convention_for_the_Conservation_of_Antarctic_Marine_Living_Resources" title="Convention for the Conservation of Antarctic Marine Living Resources">Convention for the Conservation of Antarctic Marine Living Resources</a> (CCAMLR) system.
</p><p>In 2001 the South Georgia government was cited by the <a href="/wiki/Marine_Stewardship_Council" title="Marine Stewardship Council">Marine Stewardship Council</a> for its sustainable Patagonian toothfish fishery, certifying that South Georgia met the MSC's environmental standards. The certificate places limits on the timing and quantity of Patagonian toothfish that may be caught.<sup id="cite_ref-Whole_Foods_32-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Whole_Foods-32">&#91;32&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Tourism">Tourism</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=15" title="Edit section: Tourism">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1097763485">.mw-parser-output .ambox{border:1px solid #a2a9b1;border-left:10px solid #36c;background-color:#fbfbfb;box-sizing:border-box}.mw-parser-output .ambox+link+.ambox,.mw-parser-output .ambox+link+style+.ambox,.mw-parser-output .ambox+link+link+.ambox,.mw-parser-output .ambox+.mw-empty-elt+link+.ambox,.mw-parser-output .ambox+.mw-empty-elt+link+style+.ambox,.mw-parser-output .ambox+.mw-empty-elt+link+link+.ambox{margin-top:-1px}html body.mediawiki .mw-parser-output .ambox.mbox-small-left{margin:4px 1em 4px 0;overflow:hidden;width:238px;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:88%;line-height:1.25em}.mw-parser-output .ambox-speedy{border-left:10px solid #b32424;background-color:#fee7e6}.mw-parser-output .ambox-delete{border-left:10px solid #b32424}.mw-parser-output .ambox-content{border-left:10px solid #f28500}.mw-parser-output .ambox-style{border-left:10px solid #fc3}.mw-parser-output .ambox-move{border-left:10px solid #9932cc}.mw-parser-output .ambox-protection{border-left:10px solid #a2a9b1}.mw-parser-output .ambox .mbox-text{border:none;padding:0.25em 0;text-align:center}.mw-parser-output .ambox .mbox-empty-cell{border:none;padding:0;width:1px}.mw-parser-output .ambox .mbox-image-div{width:52px}html.client-js body.skin-minerva .mw-parser-output .mbox-text-span{margin-left:23px!important}@media(min-width:720px){.mw-parser-output .ambox{margin:0 10%}}</style><table class="box-Unreferenced_section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/50px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/75px-Question_book-new.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/100px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a class="external text" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">February 2015</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this template message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p><a href="/wiki/Tourism" title="Tourism">Tourism</a> has become a larger source of income in recent years, with many cruise ships and sailing yachts visiting the area (the only way to visit South Georgia is by sea; there are no airstrips on the Islands). The territory gains income from landing charges and the sale of souvenirs. <a href="/wiki/Cruise_ship" title="Cruise ship">Cruise ships</a> often combine a Grytviken visit with a trip to the <a href="/wiki/Antarctic_Peninsula" title="Antarctic Peninsula">Antarctic Peninsula</a>.
</p><p>Charter yacht visits usually begin in the Falkland Islands, last between four and six weeks, and enable guests to visit remote harbours of South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands. Sailing vessels are now required to anchor out and can no longer tie up to the old whaling piers on shore. One exception to this is the recently upgraded/repaired yacht berth at Grytviken. All other jetties at former whaling stations lie inside a 200&#160;m (656&#160;ft) exclusion zone; and berthing, or putting ropes ashore, at these is forbidden. Yachts visiting South Georgia are normally expected to report to the Government Officers at King Edward Point before moving round the island.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Postage_stamps">Postage stamps</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=16" title="Edit section: Postage stamps">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"/><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Postage_stamps_and_postal_history_of_South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands" title="Postage stamps and postal history of South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands">Postage stamps and postal history of South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands</a></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1097763485"/><table class="box-Unreferenced_section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><a href="/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/50px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" width="50" height="39" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/75px-Question_book-new.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/100px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" /></a></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a class="external text" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit">improve this section</a> by <a href="/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">July 2010</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a href="/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this template message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:222px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Stamp_South_Georgia_1963_4d.jpg" class="image"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/c/c3/Stamp_South_Georgia_1963_4d.jpg/220px-Stamp_South_Georgia_1963_4d.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="135" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/c/c3/Stamp_South_Georgia_1963_4d.jpg/330px-Stamp_South_Georgia_1963_4d.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/c/c3/Stamp_South_Georgia_1963_4d.jpg 2x" data-file-width="403" data-file-height="248" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Stamp_South_Georgia_1963_4d.jpg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>This postage stamp depicting a <a href="/wiki/Fin_whale" title="Fin whale">fin whale</a> was issued in 1963.</div></div></div>
<p>A large source of income from abroad also comes from the issue of South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands postage stamps which are produced in the UK.
</p><p>A reasonable issue policy (few sets of stamps are issued each year) along with attractive subject matter (especially whales) makes them popular with <a href="/wiki/Topical_stamp_collecting" title="Topical stamp collecting">topical stamp collectors</a>.
</p><p>There are only four genuine <a href="/wiki/First_day_cover" class="mw-redirect" title="First day cover">first day cover</a> sets from 16 March 1982 in existence. They were stamped at the South Georgia Post Office; all those in circulation were stamped elsewhere and sent out, but the only genuine ones were kept at the Post Office on South Georgia. These four sets were removed during the Falklands War by a member of staff of the British Antarctic Survey in the few moments the Argentinians allowed them to gather their belongings. Everything else was burnt, but these four sets were saved and brought to the UK by Robert Headland, BAS.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Currency">Currency</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=17" title="Edit section: Currency">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"/><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/British_currency_in_the_South_Atlantic_and_the_Antarctic" class="mw-redirect" title="British currency in the South Atlantic and the Antarctic">British currency in the South Atlantic and the Antarctic</a></div>
<p>The <a href="/wiki/Pound_sterling" title="Pound sterling">pound sterling</a> is the official currency of the islands, and the same notes and coins are used as in the United Kingdom.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Internet_domain_registration">Internet domain registration</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=18" title="Edit section: Internet domain registration">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>The <a href="/wiki/Internet" title="Internet">Internet</a> <a href="/wiki/Country_code" title="Country code">country code</a> <a href="/wiki/Top-level_domain" title="Top-level domain">top-level domain</a> (<a href="/wiki/CcTLD" class="mw-redirect" title="CcTLD">ccTLD</a>) for South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands is <a href="/wiki/.gs" title=".gs">.gs</a>.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Ecology">Ecology</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands&amp;action=edit&amp;section=19" title="Edit section: Ecology">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<table style="margin:1em auto;">
